# NZAMY — Production-readiness inventory (appendix to the plan) — 2026-09-25

**What this is.** The raw, verified inventory behind [`docs/plans/PRODUCTION_READINESS_PLAN_2026-09-25.md`](../plans/PRODUCTION_READINESS_PLAN_2026-09-25.md). It was produced on 2026-09-25 at HEAD `1645676` by a 35-agent read-only audit (11 dashboard readers each followed by an adversarial verifier, a profiles/registration reader, three API-route readers, a migrations-vs-live-schema reader, two cross-cutting readers, two backlog re-verifiers, and two owner-question collectors merged and refuted). Every claim cites `file:line`; "live" means present in `scripts/selfhost/run4_delivery/01-schema.sql` (the schema production runs on) plus `20260925_04`. Nothing here was edited by hand — read it as evidence, and read the plan for the decisions. Owner questions are in `أسئلة_المالك_المجمّعة_٢٠٢٦-٠٩-٢٥.md` (Arabic, repo root).

## A. Dashboard pages — every `page.tsx` under `src/app/dashboard/<role>/` (207 pages)

Status vocabulary: **real** (renders API/DB data, no fake fallback) · **mock-as-real** (hardcoded data shown as fact) · **local-only-disclosed** (local state, disclosed on screen) · **honest-coming-soon** (`DashboardComingSoon`) · **hybrid** · **demo-fallback** (real fetch, failure swallowed into fixtures) · **broken** · **redirect-or-empty**. Statuses are the verifier-corrected ones. Size: S < ½ day · M 1–2 days · L 3+ days.

Cross-page defects found by the verifier (business):
- cases/page.tsx:251 + clientWorkflowRepository.ts:319-320,341: the requester filter runs in the browser and drops every colleague's business_id row. The route deliberately leaves those rows to RLS (service-requests/route.ts:161-169; policy 01-schema.sql:10807). So «قضايا الشركة» is per-login, while t
- cases/page.tsx:538: SubscriptionGuard 'business-litigation' requires tier 'pro' in production (featureAccess.ts:38; SubscriptionGuard.tsx:27-60). A lower-tier company gets «هذه الميزة مقفلة» instead of its own real requests. The same tier gates hide pages in hearings (:57, 'hearings'=pro), employee-
- cases/[id]/page.tsx:151-160 opens attachments through browser-side storage signing (documentService.ts:480-489). The storage.objects SELECT policy for bucket 'documents' only admits the uploader's own folder (01-schema.sql:14905,14909). Files uploaded by the fulfilment team or a colleague fail with 
- /api/v1/lawyer/case-graph/[caseId] (used by the cases/[id] graph tab): the PUT upsert (route.ts:143-157) never checks that the caller can see service_requests caseId. The INSERT policy only checks owner_user_id = auth.uid() (01-schema.sql:10889), and the FK (:8835) bypasses RLS. Whichever party crea
- The case-graph route returns English and raw DB errors: 'caseId required' (:53,:116), error.message (:64,:161), 'Internal server error' (:82,:167), 'nodes and edges must both be arrays' (:127). This breaks the {error:'<Arabic>'} rule.
- service-requests routes, which back the overview, cases list, case file and AddCaseModal, return English and raw errors. route.ts: :129 'Unauthorized' (GET), :257 'Unauthorized' (POST), :493 raw reqError.message/code/hint, :673 'Internal server error'. [id]/route.ts: :199, :422 and :1183 'Unauthoriz
- documents/route.ts:204 returns the raw DB error.message, code and hint on POST. copy/route.ts:48 returns 'Unauthorized' and :118 returns raw insertError.message. The reader listed only :120, :128 and :210.
- documents/page.tsx: the «company vault» is per-login. GET filters owner_user_id = user.id (documents/route.ts:47,53), so a legal_manager or other member admitted by entityRouteAccess.ts:41 sees an empty vault for the same company.
- documents/page.tsx:165-166 overclaims «وأرفقها مع أي طلب لاحق بنقرة». VaultAttachPicker has one call site (src/app/dashboard/client/requests/new/page.tsx:270), and the overview's AddCaseModal has no attach field. The overview already corrected this same claim (page.tsx:511-523).
- AddCaseModal.tsx:58 «(PDPL)» and :65 «(HR)» put English into rendered option labels, violating the Arabic-only UI rule.
- layout.tsx:50-80: SectionNotReady («هذا القسم قيد الإعداد») is a second placeholder component outside DashboardComingSoon. It and the service-mode banner also carry English fallback branches (:61,:66,:76,:129).
- cases/new/page.tsx:100,107,188-196: the copy is written for a law firm (نظام المحاماة, clients of the office, «اسم الموكل المحتمل», a billing-model picker) and is shown to a company.
- seconded-counsel/page.tsx:273: «تمثيل في النزاعات» links to /dashboard/business/cases, a hidden section that renders SectionNotReady for corporate users. The reader did not list this link.
- hearings/page.tsx:24: English «منصة ZATCA»; team/[id]/page.tsx:109 «هيئة Zakat», :120 and :135 «PDPL». These are English in user-facing copy.
- ai/procedures/page.tsx (the procedures-expert re-export): «البيانات تُحدَّث دورياً» (:374) and «استشر الذكاء الاصطناعي عن الإجراءات» (:395) are false claims over a keyword matcher.
- employee-contracts/page.tsx:271: showRiskFilter is never used and filterRisk has no UI. The :338 «تحتاج معالجة» tile counts expired+flagged but filters only 'expired'.
- Literal «قريباً» matches outside DashboardComingSoon, low severity: employee-contracts/page.tsx:337 and team/[id]/page.tsx:346,:417 («تنتهي قريباً»). These are expiry-status labels, not coming-soon placeholders.
- CaseGraphView.tsx:147: saveCaseGraph persists only nodes, edges and viewport. The groups state (_use-case-graph-state.ts:64) is dropped, so after reload nodes keep a groupId pointing at no group (CaseGraphView.tsx:429) and group label, colour and lock are lost without notice.
- The reader's dead_links entry for businessProfileReadiness.ts overstates the problem. The file is imported only by src/app/dashboard/admin/business/page.tsx, so its route references are admin-facing, not corporate dead links.
- Stale comments that mislead the next editor: cases/page.tsx:19-22 (claims RLS admits only requester/assignee) and :56-60 (claims [id] is still fabricated); kanban/page.tsx:16-19 (claims CaseGraphView is unimported); team/page.tsx:170 (claims no invitation is sent).
- Line reference fix: MyMarketplaceDashboard.tsx gray-200 hover traps are at :168, :181, :194 and :351 (the reader cited :195).

- In the arbitrator overview, «فتح الملف» on every case row links back to the list /dashboard/provider/arbitration/cases, not to that case's file (ArbitratorDashboard.tsx:272-277).
- Calendar week navigation is broken. getWeekDates steps 5 days per 'week' (calendar/page.tsx:62-68, offset*5) while the day labels are fixed Sunday to Thursday (DAY_AR :70). «الأسبوع التالي» therefore starts on Friday 10 April labelled «الأحد», and the labels drift further on each click.

- Profile Escrow copy says the money is paid «للعميل» (profile/page.tsx:355), but the provider is the payee. The «×١.٧٥» instant multiplier (:359) does not match its own fees 549/299 = ×1.84 (:46-47). The rating row fills 4 stars for «٤.٨» (:384,387).

- Dynamic Tailwind interpolation `hover:${isDark ? "bg-white/2" : "bg-gray-50/70"}` means the hover classes are never generated (page.tsx:191,265; ArbitratorDashboard.tsx:239; arbitration/files/page.tsx:154).

### B.individual
**Gaps:**
- Forced onboarding redo after full email registration because the trigger never writes onboarding_completed (01-schema.sql:697-722 vs register/client/page.tsx:302).
- Preferences (language/theme/calendar) never reach profiles columns (ProfileTab.tsx:406-408) despite columns and allowlist existing.
- No avatar upload anywhere (ProfileTab.tsx:322-325; only 'blog-covers' and 'documents' buckets exist, 01-schema.sql:14898-14899).
- No identity document (national ID) on file by design (register/client/page.tsx:303-323) — ProfileTab.tsx:398-402 says the team reviews «رقم الهوية والوثائق» yet no screen collects either.
- Registration offers non-Saudi countries (register/client/components/Steps.tsx:403-412) while phone validation accepts Saudi mobiles only (saudiMobile.ts:84-85; DB CHECK 01-schema.sql:4308) — a UAE/EG/JO registrant cannot complete signup.
- profiles.display_name_en never editable for any type (allowlisted route.ts:439 but no field spec).

**Needed:**
- [S] Write onboarding_completed (from raw_user_meta_data) in handle_new_user for email signups, or have /register/client PATCH it post-signup — stops the wizard redo (migration + trigger edit)
- [S] Persist language/theme/calendar_type to profiles via existing PATCH allowlist (ProfileTab.tsx:409-486)
- [M] Avatar upload: Storage bucket 'avatars' + RLS + POST /api/v1/profile/avatar + ProfileTab control; make useUser read profiles.avatar_url not user_metadata (useUser.ts:802)
- [M] Identity document collection for manual verification (or delete the ProfileTab.tsx:398-402 sentence) — *owner decision:* Does the platform store a national ID / ID document for individuals at all (PDPL retention)? Comment at register/client/page.tsx:303-323 says this needs a legal owner.
- [M] Either drop non-SA countries from registration or accept international mobiles (validator + CHECK + trigger clamp) — *owner decision:* Is the platform Saudi-only at launch? If yes remove AE/EG/JO/KW/QA/BH/OM/MA from Steps.tsx:403-412 and Steps.tsx:204-213 (provider).

## C. API routes and service modules (294 entries)

Only entries with a finding are listed (an entry with no issue, live schema OK, not a stub and with callers is omitted). Columns: auth = none / user / role-gated / admin / cron-secret / webhook-secret; client = rls / service / both / none.

### C.be-user-domain

**Top issues:**
- drafts/cart PUT destroys the user's saved cart. It deletes all rows with no transaction (drafts/cart/route.ts:66-69), then inserts article_title (:77), a column not present in live law_draft_carts (01-schema.sql:3709-3721). Every non-empty save wipes the cart and returns a raw error (:86). This is the one unbacked route in the group.
- A counterparty can permanently destroy the other party's documents. documents/[id] DELETE ?permanent=1 hard-deletes the storage object and row through the service role after only an RLS visibility check (documents/[id]/route.ts:61-65, :86-103). The attachments select and update policies admit both participants of a request (01-schema.sql:10704, :10722), so a participant can trash, lift the hold on (hold/route.ts:77-85) and then purge the other side's file. The same scope lets a participant publish the other side's 
- lawyer/finance POST takes requestId from the body with no ownership check and inserts payments through the service role against any service request (finance/route.ts:347, :389-401). Without requestId it fabricates completed service_requests rows through the service role (:343-365). The route also has English errors and raw DB messages (:370, :405, :465, :515), and partial collections overwrite rather than accumulate (:496-498).
- service-requests POST trusts client-supplied assigned_to, id and metadata (service-requests/route.ts:464, :471, :475) and lets an order start at in_review (:359-370). A client can pre-assign an order to any lawyer, and the consultation trigger propagates that lawyer. The route leaks the PG message, code and hint (:493), and the paid path inserts payments.status='pending' (:648), which payments_status_check rejects (01-schema.sql:4257).
- Groups are broken for anyone who isn't the owner under live RLS. Join can never find a group by code, because SELECT is members only (01-schema.sql:11744), the join insert is owner-only (01-schema.sql:11760), and codes are never set on create (groups/join/route.ts:33-44, :68-82; groups/route.ts:102-110). The invite route returns a code a non-owner never saved (invite/route.ts:62-70). The member list shows no names because profiles RLS is own-row only (members/route.ts:27-32).
- Chat creation fails against the live schema. POST accepts room_type 'support', which the chat_rooms CHECK rejects (chat/rooms/route.ts:125-130). It inserts with .select() before any participant exists under a participants-only SELECT policy (:133-143; 01-schema.sql:11042), and leaves orphan rooms. Plausible: the chat_participants SELECT policy is self-referential (01-schema.sql:12387), which risks 42P17 recursion on every chat read.
- /api/v1/cases and /api/v1/cases/[id] read public.cases, which has no writer in src/ or the live schema, so the client 'my cases' page and CasePicker are always empty (cases/route.ts:30-35; casesService.ts:105,124). /cases/[id], /consultations/[id], /groups/[id] and /lawyer/work-sessions/[id] have no callers.
- The service client is used on per-user authorization and read paths against the house rule. service-requests/[id]/attachments (:26-44) and deliverable (:23-41) hand-roll their authorization, and attachments also signs trashed files (:66-70). lawyer/finance PATCH reads through the service role before its ownership check (:457-478). The service role is also used for profile, email and phone hydration in lawyer/clients (:242), consultations/_shared.ts:159, contracts/_shared.ts:213, cases notes _shared.ts:87, reviews/r
- Error-contract violations run through the whole group. Raw Postgres messages are returned at roughly 45 sites, including case-graph:64 and :161; case-stages:119, :209 and :449; tasks:115, :230, :376, :405 and :416; hearings:122 and :208; cases:46; consultations:44; documents:204; copy:118; service-requests:493; [id]:1049; events:75 and :165; firm/activity:50 and :75; groups, join, invite and members; settings:28 and :104; notifications:44, :90 and :106; drafts/cart:30 and :86; chat:46, :63, :143 and :163. English b
- The legal-deadline radar can silently miss or miscompute deadlines. case-stages PATCH auto-creates the appeal or cassation deadline only when outcome is sent in the same request (case-stages/[caseId]/route.ts:453), contrary to its contract (:43-45). lawyer/deadlines POST computes statutory due dates with no holidays when the court_holidays read fails, and still marks them computed_by_rule (deadlines/route.ts:268-274, :297-303). Changing reminder offsets never re-queues the outbox (deadlines/[id]/route.ts:195-208).

### C.be-admin-platform

**Top issues:**
- Admin authority is unsafe. POST /api/v1/admin/teams with an existing account's email silently promotes it to full admin and overwrites its profile and metadata (src/app/api/v1/admin/teams/route.ts:99-142). It then reports «تم إرسال الدعوة بنجاح» although no email or password is ever issued (:88-95,:150). 'Suspend' revokes nothing, in teams PATCH (:198-212) or in users/[id] PATCH, which only cancels subscriptions (src/app/api/v1/admin/users/[id]/route.ts:213-229). users/[id] PATCH also accepts any body user_type, in
- Almost no audit trail. In this group only verifications/[id] (:145), community/moderation/[id] (:116) and community/reports/[id] (:94) write admin_audit_events, and verifications hardcodes before_state='pending' (:151). User delete (users/[id]:288), subscription, credit, entitlement, settings (payments_gateway and library_status), coupon, team and library-delete actions leave no trace.
- Double grants of entitlements. admin/entitlements/requests/[id] updates without .eq('status','pending') (:73-76,:111-114), so concurrent approvals grant credits or wallet twice. invite/[code]/accept grants before an unchecked status flip (:150-188), so one invitation can be redeemed by several users. library/invitations/redeem keeps no per-user record, so one account can re-redeem a multi-use code to extend Pro indefinitely (redeem/route.ts:79-173 plus src/lib/entitlementGrantRules.ts:160).
- Subscription writes are not transactional and accept invalid values. admin/subscriptions POST cancels every active plan before the insert, with the cancel unchecked (:268-276), and accepts shield/enterprise (:186), which subscriptions_tier_check rejects (schema 4639). subscriptions/[id] PATCH invents plan_id `${audience}-${tier}` (:116), which fails the FK (schema 10067). grantEntitlement with replaceActive does the same cancel-then-insert (src/lib/entitlements.ts:214-236). A failure leaves the user with no plan.
- Community counters and access are broken for real users. vote_count and view_count are updated under RLS, where only the author may UPDATE (schema 10752,10759), so votes and views never register (vote/route.ts:75-86,:111-122; posts/[id]/route.ts:67-70). Post detail returns 401 to guests although the list is public (posts/[id]/route.ts:49-51). The lawyers_only tab is always empty because of the RLS SELECT policy (schema 10629). category is never set (posts/route.ts:91-97).
- Admin verification runs on fabricated evidence. GET /api/v1/admin/verifications lists «بطاقة هوية» and other documents that were never checked or uploaded, plus a heuristic 'aiScore' (verifications/route.ts:205-256). Its paging is also wrong: the per-table range is merged, total = result.length, and rows are sorted by id string (:56,:73,:90,:180-182).
- Money and KPI figures are wrong or silently truncated. admin/stats ignores all five query errors, so zeros appear as real numbers (stats/route.ts:51-94), and it counts admin-granted subscriptions as MRR (:110-127), as do erp and corporates. erp invents rating 4.5 and city «الرياض» (erp/route.ts:72-74). Unranged reads are capped at PostgREST max-rows: revenue (:55-73), stats (:64-87), marketplace, which reads all profiles (:21), erp (:20-33), and the broadcast fan-out (src/lib/broadcastFanout.ts:50). broadcasts/[id]
- Dangerous or dead public endpoints. POST /api/v1/n8n/trigger (unused) lets any request participant fire arbitrary n8n events, including service_request.completed, which sends a «طلبك جاهز» WhatsApp for an unfinished order (trigger/route.ts:70-72,:89). academy/vault-explorer reads hardcoded D:\ paths, so the academy vault is dead in production and returns English errors that leak paths (vault-explorer/route.ts:5-6,:17,:44,:97). Eight routes have no in-app caller: academy attempts/categories/questions, ai-review-requ
- Client-facing data defects. research/desktop can create duplicate '__desktop__' sessions under concurrency: the maybeSingle error is ignored and there is no unique constraint (desktop/route.ts:12-29), after which the desktop stays empty. That session also shows in the sessions list (sessions/route.ts:29-34). /api/v1/wallet shows every active coupon to every user regardless of eligibility (wallet/route.ts:219-223; policy schema 10573). /api/v1/referrals hands out a referral link that nothing records or honours (refe
- Contract hygiene violations across roughly 40 routes. English error bodies appear in community/*, research/*, academy/*, n8n/*, promo, referrals, wallet, contact:35, cron:123 and admin/community/moderation/[id]:140 ('Internal server error'). Raw Postgres or GoTrue error.message is leaked in admin tickets, articles, coupons, receipts, subscriptions, users, settings, teams, verifications and library, and in entitlement-requests:56 and wallet:172. `.single()` makes the 404 branches dead, returning 500 instead (tickets

A transport fault then reads as signed-out. |

Low risk, but the service role signs whatever path is stored. |

### C.be-library-ai-services

**Top issues:**
- Smart-folder writes cannot work on the live schema. library.smart_folders and library.smart_folder_items grant only SELECT to authenticated (01-schema.sql:13516-13527; default privileges 14804-14805), so POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/library/folders (route.ts:72-87, 122-134, 192-216) and POST /api/library/folders/items (route.ts:53-73) always fail 42501 → 500 with an English body. Folders can be read but never created or filled in production.
- POST /api/library/reports is dead and broken. library.issue_reports has a SELECT-only grant (01-schema.sql:13498-13499), and its insert policy requires user_id = auth.uid() (10292) while guests insert null (route.ts:38), so every report fails with a 500. The route has no callers; the working path is /api/v1/library/issue-reports → public.library_issue_reports. It should be removed.
- /api/ai/library-chat and /api/ai/explain-article are anonymous and unmetered. They check no session, tier or credits, rely only on a per-process in-memory IP limit of 20/min (library-chat/route.ts:26-33; explain-article/route.ts:25-32), and call n8n with no timeout. explain-article also passes n8n's relatedArticles[].text (statutory text) through with no paywall check (route.ts:20-21, 90-91). It has no live caller: the UI fakes a «قريباً» reply instead (_ai-components.tsx:278-285).
- User data is kept in localStorage in live mode. researchService getInbox/clearBySource/clearDesktop/clearAll/mergeItems never call the server (researchService.ts:298, 391-399). DesktopPanel shows «تم مسح الديسك توب» (DesktopPanel.tsx:149) while the server rows remain, even though DELETE /api/v1/research/desktop exists. removeFromInbox, updateItem and markUsed swallow failures (360-389). useClientGroupMembership seeds group membership from localStorage and falls back to it (useClientGroupMembership.ts:17, 25).
- entitlements.grantEntitlement is not atomic. It cancels active subscriptions with an unchecked error before the insert (entitlements.ts:205-232). Credits are a read-modify-write with an unchecked lawyer_profiles update (254-294). Raw Postgres messages are returned (124, 232, 287, 320, 327). VALID_TIERS includes shield/enterprise, which the DB tier checks reject (01-schema.sql:4608, 4639).
- Service-role is used for per-user reads on every paywalled library route. getUserTier reads the caller's public.subscriptions row with createServiceClient (access-control.ts:94-107), via checkLibraryAccess/getLibraryAccessForUser from search:162, init:70, laws:178, books:76, decrees:40 and precedents:63-64 (twice per request). It is not an IDOR, because userId comes from a verified getUser, but it breaks the RLS-for-user-reads rule.
- GET /api/library/enactments skips libraryGate() (enactments/route.ts:10-12), so it keeps serving library.laws metadata while platform_settings.library_status is closed, unlike every other library content route. Its list body {data, source} has no total (route.ts:52).
- /api/client-pricing reads public.admin_pricing_catalog with SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY (route.ts:8-9, 21-27) although the anon RLS policy 'public read enabled individual pricing' exists (01-schema.sql:12495). It silently serves the hard-coded CLIENT_SERVICE_CATALOG when env is missing or on a non-2xx answer (route.ts:11-19, 32). The fetch is unwrapped, so a network error returns HTML instead of JSON. The body is not {data,total}.
- Library detail routes can fail silently or entirely. decrees/[id] never reads the decree_pages error (route.ts:46-51), so a failed read is a 200 with an empty decree. It maps every lookup error to an English 404 'Decree not found' (29-31, 89). laws/[slug] throws on any article/regulation status outside 7 tokens (route.ts:317-329), but library.articles.status and article_regulations.status are unconstrained varchar (01-schema.sql:1306, 1281), so one bad row returns a 500 for the whole law.
- The service layer truncates lists silently and leaks English errors. serviceOrders.listMyServiceOrders (serviceOrders.ts:125) and workflowService.getWorkflowRequests* (workflowService.ts:65, 76) send no limit, so they get the route default of 20 (service-requests/route.ts:133) and ignore total. English text reaches the UI through api.ts defaults 'Request failed'/'API error: N' (api.ts:60-61, 84-85), WorkflowApiError (clientWorkflowRepository.ts:65), documentService 'Unauthorized' (366), and raw storage messages (do

Notes:
- Classification rule: applied = the durable effects are live, and every missing piece is removed or renamed by a named later file (or by the drop-every-policy loop at 20260921_03:214-235). superseded = nothing distinctive survives because a later file replaced all of it. partially-applied = something is missing with no later file to explain it; no file fell into this class. code_dependents is filled only on entries that are not live.
- Coverage: 82 entries = 81 files in supabase/migrations/ (77 dated migrations, 2 _superseded_, 1 _staging_only_, _verify.sql) plus supabase/storage_policies_documents.sql. Nothing was skipped.
- Summary: 60 applied, 8 not-applied (20260820_academy, 20260821_feqh_is_synthetic_page, 20260822_needs_human_review, 20260919_private_precedent, 20260823_fts_number_search, 20260824_articles_number_text_widen, and storage_policies_documents.sql), 2 do-not-apply (20260925_01 and _staging_only_), 3 superseded (both _superseded_ files and 20260824_laws_effective_date_gregorian_columns), 8 data-only-or-unverifiable.
- Batch C (20260820, 20260821_feqh_blocks_is_synthetic_page, 20260822_needs_human_review_columns, 20260919_private_precedent_details) is confirmed not live, and none of it has a runtime dependent in src/. The academy runs on static data. The search route's use of 20260925_01 is a guarded fallback (route.ts:240-262), so its absence is the supported state and it is excluded from unapplied_with_dependents.
- unapplied_with_dependents lists degraded features, not HTTP errors. For 20260823, searching principles or decrees by printed number returns no hits (search/route.ts:423, :478). For 20260824_articles_number_text_widen, number_text is capped at varchar(50) (01-schema.sql:1304).
- 20260823_fts_number_search cannot be applied as written. It drops the fts column on decrees_circulars and principles (L67, L87) without first dropping the matview library.cross_section_search, which selects p.fts and dc.fts (01-schema.sql:1854, 1864). The drop fails with a dependency error and the transaction rolls back. It must drop and recreate the matview, as 20260729:59-65 does.
- Banner accuracy: the 'NOT APPLIED YET' banner on 20260824_laws_effective_date_gregorian_columns (L31) is stale; its columns are live through 20260911. The banners on 20260823 (L42) and 20260824_articles_number_text_widen (L32) are accurate.
- Live defect with no migration behind it: authenticated holds only SELECT on library.smart_folders, smart_folder_items and issue_reports (01-schema.sql:13498-13500, 13516-13527). No migration grants writes on them (20260626:885 and 20260922_01:170 grant SELECT only). The anon-key RLS client writes to them anyway, at src/app/api/library/folders/route.ts:72-82, :123-125, :193-209; folders/items/route.ts:54-56; and reports/route.ts:35-37. Each of these writes should fail with 42501 on production (the English error text
- public.rls_auto_enable (01-schema.sql:1094) exists with no migration behind it, and the dump contains no event trigger. Nothing on the self-host enables RLS on new public tables automatically.
- scripts/seed-library.ts in --apply-live mode writes the batch-C columns and calls store_private_precedent_details (:674, :894, :1199, :1250, :1369, :1414), so it would fail against production. scripts/seed-library-from-owner.mjs:191-204 strips those columns, but does not truncate number_text. Whether any article's number_text exceeds 50 characters in the owner package cannot be determined from the repo; such a row would fail its batch with 22001.
- Data seeds cannot be verified from a schema-only dump. copy-reference-data.mjs covers jurisdictions, blog_sections, admin_pricing_catalog, court_holidays, deadline_rules, platform_settings (missing keys only), subscription_plans and credit_packages. The coupons seeded by 20260603_003 are copied only with --with-coupons (copy-reference-data.mjs:207), so they may be missing on the clean start. The payments_gateway value should be checked with _verify.sql:332-364.
- Every other migration object is accounted for in the dump, including those created through execute format: the 15 'Allow public read on library.*' policies (20260626:791) and the 4 invitee policies (20260922_02:212-237). The realtime publication members (01-schema.sql:14916-14917) and the buckets documents and blog-covers (:14898-14899) match their migrations.

Summary:
- Inventory: all 76 src/app/ai/**/page.tsx files are covered below, one entry each. 8 work and are server-backed: six order flows that end in a manual-fulfilment order (draft, contracts, wargaming, memo-studio which re-exports wargaming, legal-opinion including its letter flow, letter-drafter), plus the orders/[id] tracker and the research collector. 8 work client-side only or are static (the hub, debt-collection, fee-calculator, najiz-optimizer, ngo/governance-checker, templates, disclaimer, and the research redirec
- No /ai page depends on n8n. None of them calls an API that forwards to process.env.N8N_*. The only AI routes that forward to n8n are /api/ai/library-chat and /api/ai/explain-article, both used from /laws. explain-article has no caller at all.
- The task assumes BetaReviewGate turns requests into service_requests rows. At HEAD it does not: no call site passes orderPayload, so every gate shows the «المخرج التلقائي غير متاح خلال البيتا، ولم يُسجَّل أي طلب» card. reviewScope='legal-data' also gates every role, not only lawyer and firm (src/components/BetaReviewGate.tsx:159-171, 403-453; src/lib/betaConfig.ts:40). Manual orders come only from the six dedicated flows: createServiceOrder or createWorkflowRequest → POST /api/v1/service-requests with receiver ai_w
- Worst live defects: invented legal content shown with no gate, and mostly reachable by logged-out visitors because only 11 /ai prefixes are protected (src/proxy.ts:110-126). /ai/monitor and /ai/corp/monitor show invented law amendments with fake gazette sources. /ai/procedures shows invented government court e-mail addresses as mailto links. /ai/legal-translate returns one fixed translation whatever you enter. /ai/transcriber in verbatim mode returns one fixed transcript. On the lawyer path, /ai/analyze shows inven
- User data kept in localStorage (a defect under the product rule): /ai/settings (C7). Via workflowStore.saveWorkflowRequest → clientWorkflowRepository key nzamy_workflow_requests_v1: contract-drafter:70, gov/judgment-drafter:32, micro:158 (a lawyer consultation marked pending_assignment that never leaves the browser), and ngo/volunteer-contract:126 (volunteer ID and phone). Separately, the collector's 7-day auto-archive only acts on the old localStorage store.
- «قريباً» wording outside DashboardComingSoon (defect): quick-answer, secretary, vault (:151, :324), global (:134), and the /laws AI chat and explain panels (src/app/laws/[slug]/_ai-components.tsx:128,133,284,428-431). In the catalog, quick-answer (20 pts) and secretary (5 pts) are priced even though their pages are unavailable. pointCost is never charged anywhere; the only writer to credit_transactions is the admin grant in src/lib/entitlements.ts:279.
- Channels: only in-app notifications work end to end (src/lib/notify.ts → public.notifications, with realtime). Email: the app sends no email of its own. Sign-up mail depends on the self-hosted GoTrue SMTP_* settings, and /forgot-password is entirely fake (no auth call). WhatsApp: the app never reads EVOLUTION_*; every automated WhatsApp message would have to go through n8n, and the code says N8N_WEBHOOK_BASE_URL is unset on the server (src/app/api/v1/admin/service-orders/[id]/route.ts:28-31), so today every dispatc
- n8n exports: there are 7 JSON files in n8n/workflows and none can be imported against the live schema as they stand. The app posts to /request-assigned and /request-completed, but wf-2.2 and wf-2.3 both listen on /request-status. No export exists for /contact, N8N_WEBHOOK_DEADLINE, library-chat, explain-article, the WhatsApp delivery notice, or a cron scheduler. The SQL uses columns and tables that do not exist live: profiles.full_name, notifications.type, audit_log, lawyer_profiles.is_verified and specialization, 
- Env vars: the code reads exactly 5 N8N vars: N8N_WEBHOOK_BASE_URL, N8N_WEBHOOK_SECRET, N8N_WEBHOOK_DEADLINE, N8N_LIBRARY_CHAT_WEBHOOK_URL and N8N_EXPLAIN_WEBHOOK_URL. .env.example declares 28 N8N_WEBHOOK_* vars that nothing reads, and it omits the last two above even though the code reads them. EVOLUTION_API_URL, EVOLUTION_API_KEY and EVOLUTION_INSTANCE_NAME are never read by the app. N8N_API_KEY (n8n/README.md:145) is unused.
- The deadline cron (/api/v1/cron/deadlines) needs CRON_SECRET and an external scheduler, and the repo contains no scheduler. Until one exists, missed-deadline marking, in-app reminders and the 30-day attachment purge never run. Its email/WhatsApp branch has no producer: enqueueDeadlineReminders only ever writes in_app.
- C2 is still open at HEAD: src/app/api/v1/academy/vault-explorer/route.ts reads a hard-coded Windows path D:\Data\..., has no authentication, and returns err.message; the four fetch sites in academy/quiz/page.tsx (368, 386, 400, 422) are unchanged. C7 is still open at HEAD: /ai/settings is unchanged.
- Scope and limits: I read the working tree. The only cited file with uncommitted changes is src/app/dashboard/admin/page.tsx, and its cited lines 553-555 are identical at HEAD. For n8n_master_guide_latest.md I read headings only, as instructed. The 11 gated gov/ngo/report mock pages were classified from their fixture constants, fake delays and gate line ranges, not from a line-by-line read of each result body. No live n8n, GoTrue or DB state was checked (no network).

Summary:
- DIRECT ANSWER: no user can buy anything today. There is no checkout, no payment-provider adapter and no payment webhook anywhere under src/app/api. src/lib/paymentAdapter.ts:20 is dead code with zero importers. src/lib/paymentGatewayPolicy.ts:29-55 resolves platform_settings.payments_gateway to 'disabled' for every stored value, including 'live'. The only exception is the staging stub, which needs status 'test' + provider 'stub' + NZAMY_ALLOW_STUB_PAYMENTS='true' + NZAMY_DEPLOYMENT_ENV local/staging. Client service
- HOW PAID ACCESS ACTUALLY HAPPENS (all manual, all admin-mediated). (a) A logged-in user clicks a /pricing or /laws/subscribe CTA, which files an entitlement_requests row (PricingCards.tsx:437-449; laws/subscribe/page.tsx:248). An admin approves it; approval is always 30 days (admin/entitlements/requests/page.tsx:73-76; entitlements.ts:105). Lawyer point top-ups are filed as kind 'plan', so an approval can never add credits. (b) An admin direct grant from /dashboard/admin/entitlements. (c) An admin-issued library in
- GATED: every payment path sits behind payments_gateway, which is fail-closed; the service-requests POST returns 402 when a paid request arrives (service-requests/route.ts:303-310). Tiers 'shield' and 'enterprise' are offered in the admin console but rejected by the live CHECK constraints (01-schema.sql:4608,4639). The individual legal-protection membership (the base of the founder offer) therefore cannot be granted even by hand.
- MOCK or FIXTURE: the business wallet, firm wallet and firm fees pages; the admin escrow page; the admin pricing console (local state only); the whole academy (no academy schema is live; invented courses, instructors, students, ratings and a fabricated certificate with a verify ID); community/lawyers; the community detail fallback (fabricated verified-lawyer answers); the public blog pre-seed fallback; points consumption (never debited); coupon redemption (none); promo links (orphan route); referrals (honest Dashboa
- FOUNDER OFFERS (docs/requirements/2026-09-15-*): nothing is implemented. client-founder-2026, business-founder-national-2026 and lawyer-founder-2026 have 0 hits in src/, supabase/ and scripts/. There are no founder_* tables in the live schema, no campaign or returnTo handling (login ignores the ?from= that proxy.ts sets), no seat reservation, capacity counter, checkout, webhook, badge, founder room, verified-review model, monthly invite cycles or content-rejection test. - LEGAL PAGES: the word 'Escrow' is gone from Terms and Refund, but both still describe the payment-hold mechanism: fees collected through platform channels and released after delivery (terms/page.tsx:24,73; refund-policy/page.tsx:28-30). They also cite features that do not exist: ZATCA Phase 2 (terms:77), in-platform e-arbitration (terms:113; refund:86), auto-renew cancellation and proration (refund:39-40), an in-account refund request screen (refund:67), and app-store apps (terms:20). The homepage still says Escrow
- LIVE-SCHEMA FACTS USED: 01-schema.sql contains no seed data; its 9 INSERTs are all inside handle_new_user at :697-811. Row-level facts are therefore not verifiable from the dump: the payments_gateway value, the subscription_plans rows (scripts/selfhost/README.md:30 claims 18 exist on self-hosted), coupons, and article counts. Tables that DO exist live: payments, platform_settings, subscriptions, subscription_plans, coupons, coupon_usage, credit_packages, credit_transactions, wallet_transactions, receipts, referrals
- SIZE SCALE used in every entry: XS < 0.5 day; S 0.5-2 days; M 3-5 days; L 1-2 weeks; XL > 2 weeks or needs an external provider plus security review/staging.
- CAPS AND EXCLUSIONS (stated explicitly). «قريباً»: all 127 raw hits were classified. Notes:
- I audited HEAD 1645676 (main). git status shows about 80 modified dashboard files, but `git diff --ignore-cr-at-eol` is empty, so the changes are line endings only and HEAD content is what was checked. `.agents/skills/` is the only untracked path.
- The live schema file scripts/selfhost/run4_delivery/01-schema.sql is gitignored (.gitignore:103), so the Grep tool cannot see it. It was searched with bash grep. Every 'live' claim cites its line numbers.
- Not verified, because running them is forbidden here: the lint error count (B17), the npm@10 `npm ci` failure (B18/D8), the timezone test failure (B18), the claim of 0 type-check errors (B19), GitHub Actions status, whether _verify.sql was run on self-hosted (DEV-1), and the actual production deploy state (DEV-3). These verdicts rest on code and config, not on execution.
- Premises made obsolete by the clean self-hosted database: the 146 UAT accounts (DEV-4), the 16 users holding two business_members rows (B10, now latent), the 4 UAT cases rows (D5), and cloud-project references in docs (E5 :79, scripts/uat/README.md:6). The defects in B10, B5, B7 and D5 are still in the code or schema.
- Priority for B5: the advisor suggested blocker because verified lawyers now exist (seed-clean-test-accounts.mjs:225). I kept medium. The only anon path I found broken is GET /api/v1/reviews?lawyer= (reviews/route.ts:178-190), which has no UI consumer. The public lawyer page (/api/v1/lawyers/[id]) and all platform_settings readers use the service client. The review's receipts example no longer applies (01-schema.sql:1
- Priority rubric: B1, B2, B6, B7 and B14 are blocker (exploitable security or integrity through PostgREST: table-level GRANT ALL on service_requests at 01-schema.sql:13124-13125 and on profiles at :14577). B7 has no exposed data yet (0 reviews on the clean database). B4 and B15 are high: they are broken session paths under GoTrue faults, which the self-hosted host makes more likely.

Notes:
- Working tree: all 80 files git reports as modified differ only in line endings (git diff --ignore-cr-at-eol is empty), so every file read equals HEAD 1645676. .agents/skills/ is untracked and was not used.
- Live-DB basis: a table, column, function or policy counts as live only if it appears in scripts/selfhost/run4_delivery/01-schema.sql (or 20260925_04). No database or network was called. .env files were not opened (SH-S10 is marked unverifiable for that reason).
- Scope of (e): all 52 🟡 and all 36 🔴 rows are listed (88 items). The ✅ (86), 🟠 (5), ⚪ (13), 🔵 (1), ⛔ (1) and ⚠️ (1) rows were skipped by design.
- Scope of (a): the §4 LIB rows judged closed are listed together in SH-LIB-CLOSED (IDs named explicitly). SH-CLOSED-S covers S1, S4, S5, S6, S8 and S9. §3 has no open items.
- Scope of (f): only about 20 representative lines of REMAINING_WORK.md / IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md were re-checked. The remaining ~75 P3/P4/P5 lines (e.g. owner modifications merge, admin subscription spec, many small UX fixes, i18n) were not individually verified. Both docs are stale (2026-07-16) and should be archived rather than trusted.
- Kind rules: owner-decision when the row names a blocking owner question (Q1 insurance, Q2 hosting/PDPL, Q3 finance, Q16 badges, Q18 deadline rules) or a product decision. ops for cron, n8n, backups, UAT and server config. Otherwise dev, with content or docs where the fix is data or a document.
- Priority rules: أمني, فقد بيانات, نظامي and وعد كاذب map to high; شكلي maps to low; — maps to medium. The level is downgraded one step when the surface is hidden at HEAD (e.g. isBetaHidden insurance plans). No item is marked blocker, because nothing found breaks production outright.
- Defects found while verifying that are not items in the source docs:
- (1) Regression from the owner package sync fbf9c58 (2026-09-20): components/marketplace/MyMarketplaceDashboard.tsx:48-78 renders fabricated marketplace listings on /dashboard/lawyer/marketplace and /dashboard/firm/marketplace. The honest gate described in MyMarketplaceDashboardData.ts and proxy.ts:251-253 is gone.
- (2) /dashboard/business/team/[id] serves mock MEMBERS_BY_ID (page.tsx:72), reachable by URL through the prefix match in navigation.sidebars.business.ts:348-356.
- (3) /ai/vault (lawyer sidebar «الخزنة», navigation.sidebars.legal.ts:66) shows mock documents and a custom «قريباً».
- (4) firm/circuits-emails shows invented @moj.gov.sa addresses plus a programmer-facing banner in the UI (page.tsx:223).
- (5) services/corporate/seconded-counsel/page.tsx:102 contains the Cyrillic word «штатный».
- (6) 32 API route files return English {error:"Unauthorized"}, e.g. api/v1/community/posts/[id]/route.ts:50. Academy routes return English errors.
- (7) The visible /pricing individual plan promises Escrow and video consultations (pricing.individuals.ts:27,34).
- (8) scripts/seed-library-from-owner.mjs:12-24 strips needs_human_review, review_reason and is_synthetic_page because migrations 20260821/20260822 are not in the live schema (grep 0). Those source values are currently lost.
- (9) localStorage still holds signed-in user data (ai/settings/page.tsx:49; laws/orders/[slug]/page.tsx:227; recent-sessions keys).
- Rule conflict: the repo's own test src/lib/services/navComingSoon.test.ts enforces nav badges «قريباً» (14 badges in navigation.sidebars*.ts). Several pages use custom «قريباً» copy instead of DashboardComingSoon (ai/secretary/page.tsx:62, ai/vault/page.tsx:151). The founder spec (FM-09) also mandates a «قريباً» fallback. Under the stated product rule all of these are defects, so the owner or developer should reconci
- Data-dependent items (LIB-18 data, chapter-order descents, free-article order, 2 missing covers) and ops claims in رسالة_للمالك_٢٠٢٦-٠٩-٢٥.md (closed ports, SMTP, hourly deadline cron, nightly backups) cannot be proven from the repo and are marked accordingly.
- (b) item 1 (apply SQL) is judged closed because all four migrations' artifacts are in the live schema dump. (d): almost the whole LIBRARY_PROGRESS_TRACKER.md is obsolete after the owner-package reseed and clean start. Only the Phase 8 SEO work (SSR/metadata/JSON-LD, DB sitemap, soft-404) and legacy 'royal' remain. The tracker itself is stale (last change 2026-07-30).
